# Joeville Image-Generation Prompt Library
> Status: production prompts authored; generated assets are tracked separately.

## JV-TWN-STL-EST-EW-DAY-V001

```text
Use case: stylized-concept
Asset type: clean scenic production plate
Primary request: extreme-wide establishing for Joeville Town in Joeville.
Scene/backdrop: Townwide; narrative use: overview, montage, geographic orientation.
Style/medium: stylized square-cell voxel/ASCII production environment matching Wizard Joe's crisp grid logic; orthogonal stepped silhouettes, matte local-color blocks, sparse highlight glyph texture, readable depth, Boulder-inspired foothill light, no photoreal microtexture.
Composition/framing: 24mm-equivalent elevated; large center-safe ensemble zone; preserve lower-floor movement and a readable horizon.
Lighting/mood: clear late-morning, emotionally warm but dialogue-readable.
Color palette: sandstone, ponderosa green, creek blue, sun gold, night indigo; rainbow only as a restrained recurring accent.
Materials/textures: regional stone, brick, weathered wood, dark steel, clear glass, block-cell foliage.
Continuity: set ID JV-TWN; west/mountain side remains screen-consistent; use approved town map and matching master architecture.
Output: 3840x2160 master, compose for 16:9; layer plan far_bg|bg|midground|character_plane|foreground|fx|lighting|shadow; filename JV-TWN-STL-EST-EW-DAY-V001.png.
Constraints: empty clean plate; signage surfaces blank for controlled graphic pass.
Avoid: no people, no characters, no Wizard Joe, no logos, no trademarked signage, no generated readable text, no warped furniture, no impossible stairs, no broken perspective, no blocked walking floor, no random castle changes, no dense visual noise behind dialogue zones, no watermark.
```
